# Counter-Witness and Mundane-Null Ledger

Every master-card row is retained below, including positive events, clean responses, structural screens, and indeterminate rows. A counter-witness narrows a claim; it does not erase a positive local event. A mundane null remains a candidate explanation unless independently discriminated.
All `mundane_null_explanations` entries are labeled `PLAUSIBLE_UNTESTED_UNLESS_SEPARATELY_DISCRIMINATED`; listing one is not a completed null test.
